Conclusion: 有结构的终端参考律是一个有前景的方向，同时需要更具表现力的相位构造和更大规模的评估。

Abstract: Standard diffusion models typically use a single time-homogeneous Gaussian terminal distribution as the reference law for generation. While this choice is analytically convenient and empirically powerful, it provides little explicit structure for data concentrated near low-dimensional manifolds, where different regions of the data distribution may correspond to distinct local geometric or semantic factors. As a result, the reverse model must recover manifold-level structure almost entirely from an unstructured terminal reference distribution.
  We propose PTL-Diffusion, a proof-of-concept diffusion framework whose forward noising process converges to a nonconstant periodic family of Gaussian terminal laws rather than to a single invariant law. Unlike a phase-conditioned DDPM, where phase information only enters the denoising network while the forward process remains unchanged, PTL-Diffusion embeds phase structure directly into the forward noising dynamics.
  The proposed construction remains close to standard denoising diffusion models: for a periodically forced Ornstein--Uhlenbeck-type forward process, we derive closed-form forward marginals, the limiting periodic Gaussian terminal family, and explicit Gaussian reverse posteriors, enabling standard noise-prediction training. We also introduce an invariant-average regularization term coupling the phase-conditioned reverse dynamics through the averaged periodic reference law. Experiments on torus and cylinder point-cloud benchmarks and the Olivetti face dataset show that PTL-Diffusion improves manifold-level distributional matching over matched DDPM baselines, reducing phase-conditioned errors, feature-space covariance errors, and nearest-neighbour manifold distances. These results suggest structured terminal reference laws as a promising direction, while motivating more expressive phase constructions and larger-scale evaluations.

</details>


### [216] [OmniGameArena: A Unified UE5 Benchmark for VLM Game Agents with Improvement Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09826)
*Mingxian Lin,Shengju Qian,Yuqi Liu,Yi-Hua Huang,Yiyu Wang,Wei Huang,Yitang Li,Fan Zhang,Zeyu Hu,Lingting Zhu,Xin Wang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了OmniGameArena基准测试平台，包含12个Unreal Engine 5实时游戏（7个单人、3个PvP、2个合作），以及IDC（改进动态曲线）评估框架，通过反射式提示优化揭示VLM智能体的冷启动表现、多轮改进曲线和技能泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏基准测试存在三个关键缺陷：仅报告单次尝试分数、局限于单人模式、缺乏统一协议来公平评估不同类型的智能体（商业VLM、开源VLM、专用游戏策略）。作者旨在填补这些空白。

Method: 1) 构建OmniGameArena：12个Unreal Engine 5新游戏，覆盖Solo(7)、PvP(3)、Coop(2)三种模式，统一动作接口；2) 提出IDC（Improvement Dynamics Curve）：利用工具型反射LLM在多轮中自主优化有界技能提示，除冷启动排行榜外，额外揭示每对（智能体、游戏）的得分演化曲线和在保留任务变体上的技能泛化行为。

Result: 报告了12个VLM智能体在冷启动排行榜上的表现，以及4个顶级智能体在IDC框架下的完整观察结果（多轮改进曲线+技能泛化测试）。

Conclusion: OmniGameArena和IDC共同提供了更全面的VLM智能体评估范式，超越了传统单次排行榜评分，从冷启动表现、多轮改进动态和技能泛化三个维度衡量智能体能力。

Abstract: Vision-language model (VLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interactive game environments. Yet game benchmarks for VLM agents typically report a single first-attempt score per (agent, game) pair, focus on single-agent Solo play, and lack unified protocols for evaluating heterogeneous agent classes (commercial VLMs, open-weight VLMs, and specialized game policies) on the same footing. We address these gaps with OmniGameArena, a real-time benchmark of twelve newly built Unreal Engine 5 games spanning Solo (7), PvP (3), and Coop (2) with unified action interfaces, and the Improvement Dynamics Curve (IDC), an agentic-reflection harness in which a tool-using reflector LLM autonomously refines a bounded skill prompt across multiple rounds. Beyond cold-start leaderboard scores, IDC exposes two additional observables for each (agent, game) pair: how the score evolves across reflection rounds, and how the learned skill behaves on held-out task variants. We report these observables for twelve VLM agents on the cold-start leaderboard and four top agents under IDC.

</details>


### [217] [Latent Spatial Memory for Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09828)
*Weijie Wang,Haoyu Zhao,Yifan Yang,Feng Chen,Zeyu Zhang,Yefei He,Zicheng Duan,Donny Y. Chen,Yuqing Yang,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Mirage，一种基于潜在空间记忆的视频世界模型框架。它通过深度引导反投影将潜在token提升到3D，并直接在扩散潜在空间中通过扭曲合成新视角，避免了像素空间重建的计算开销和信息损失。实验表明，相比显式3D基线，该方法实现高达10.57倍更快的端到端视频生成和55倍的内存占用减少，在WorldScore上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频世界模型依赖RGB空间中的显式点云记忆，需要反复渲染和VAE编码，计算昂贵且存在信息损失（潜在表示通过像素空间的往返会丢失丰富特征）。本文旨在消除这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出潜在空间记忆（Latent Spatial Memory），直接在扩散潜在空间中构建持久3D缓存：通过深度引导反投影将潜在token提升到3D空间，查询时通过直接潜在空间扭曲合成新视角，避免像素空间重建。

Result: 相比显式3D基线，端到端视频生成速度提升最多10.57倍，内存占用减少55倍；在WorldScore上达到SOTA，在RealEstate10K上保持强重建质量。

Conclusion: 潜在空间记忆统一了记忆构建与查询的格式，消除了像素空间重建的信息损失和计算负担，验证了在扩散潜在空间中直接维护3D空间一致性的可行性及优势。

Abstract: Video world models that maintain 3D spatial consistency across generated frames typically rely on explicit point cloud memory constructed in RGB space. This design is both computationally expensive, requiring repeated rendering and VAE encoding, and inherently lossy, as the round trip through pixel space discards rich features of the learned latent representation. In this paper, we introduce \emph{latent spatial memory} for video world models, a persistent 3D cache that stores scene information directly in the diffusion latent space, avoiding pixel-space reconstruction. Building on this, we propose Mirage, a latent-space spatial memory framework that constructs the memory by lifting latent tokens into 3D via depth-guided back-projection and queries it by synthesizing novel views through direct latent-space warping. This unified formulation eliminates both the information loss of pixel-space reconstruction and the computational burden of repeated encoding and rendering. Experiments show that latent spatial memory achieves up to \textbf{10.57}$\times$ faster end-to-end video generation and \textbf{55}$\times$ reduction in memory footprint relative to explicit 3D baselines. Leveraging the geometric prior of the diffusion model, Mirage attains state-of-the-art performance on WorldScore and strong reconstruction quality on RealEstate10K.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [218] [Conan-embedding-v3: Fusing Modality-Specific Models for Omni-Modal Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09331)
*Shiyu Li,Zhiyuan Hu,Yifan Wang,Peiming Li,Zheng Wei,Yang Tang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: Conan-embedding-v3提出了一种解耦-融合-恢复框架，通过独立训练模态专家、融合任务向量、修复投影仪漂移问题，实现了支持文本、图像、视频、文档和音频的统一全模态检索，在MMEB上达到74.9分，在MAEB音频套件上达到55.61分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同模态（文本、图像、视频、文档、音频）在数据分布、架构和优化动态上存在显著差异，构建统一的嵌入空间进行全模态检索极具挑战性。

Method: 提出解耦-融合-恢复框架：(1) 独立训练各模态专家；(2) 通过解耦专家融合（Decoupled Specialist Fusion）将任务向量融合到单一密集骨干中；(3) 针对投影仪漂移问题，采用投影仪恢复（全参数微调投影仪，冻结骨干）和平衡多模态回放进行修复。

Result: 在MMEB基准上达到74.9分，在30任务的MAEB音频套件上达到55.61分。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了单一骨干网络支持多种模态检索，并发现了投影仪漂移这一关键失效模式及其修复策略。

Abstract: Omni-modal retrieval promises a single embedding space for text, image, video, document, and audio inputs, but building such a unified retriever is difficult since these modalities differ in data distribution, architecture, and optimization dynamics. In this work, we present Conan-embedding-v3, a decouple--fuse--recover framework for omni-modal retrieval. Conan-embedding-v3 first trains modality specialists independently and fuses their task vectors into a single dense backbone, a strategy we call Decoupled Specialist Fusion. We show that this fusion composes visual, video, and document retrieval capabilities, but also exposes a failure mode for projector-based modalities: when audio is attached through an external encoder and projector, fusing the backbone leaves the projector calibrated to the audio-specialist backbone, causing a large audio retrieval regression despite copying all audio-specific modules unchanged. We call this failure Projector Drift. To repair it, Conan-embedding-v3 applies Projector Recovery (i.e., full-parameter fine-tuning of the projector while keeping the backbone frozen) followed by balanced multi-modal rehearsal. The resulting model supports these retrieval pathways in one backbone, achieving 74.9 scores on MMEB while obtaining 55.61 on the 30-task MAEB audio suite.

</details>


### [219] [LangRetrieval: Language-Guided Self-Evolving Satellite-to-Radar Retrieval via CSI-Driven Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09486)
*Chunlei Shi,Junming Hou,Yi-Lin Wei,Jiong Wang,Yecheng Zhang,Yichao Dong,Wenqi Ren,Dan Niu*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出LangRetrieval，一种语言引导的条件流匹配框架，通过语义预热和自我进化语义优化两个核心组件，实现气象语义与卫星-雷达降水反演精度的闭环优化，有效解决了S2R反演中的病态问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的S2R反演方法由于仅依赖局部光谱线索，难以区分云顶辐射相似但降水特征不同的气象状态，而传统静态语义条件无法适应动态对流场景并与反演目标对齐，因此需要一种能够动态演化并与反演精度协同优化的语义引导机制。

Method: LangRetrieval包含两个核心模块：(i)语义预热——将结构化气象属性通过交叉注意力注入CFM骨干网络，实现沿生成轨迹的连续语义引导；(ii)自演化语义优化——先由视觉语言模型初始化轻量级属性策略，再通过GRPO结合多阈值CSI奖励进行细化，使语义生成直接向提高反演精度的方向演化。

Result: 通过GRPO和多阈值CSI奖励优化，语义生成能够直接演化以提升反演精度，验证了闭环优化机制的有效性。

Conclusion: LangRetrieval通过语言引导的条件流匹配框架，建立了气象语义与反演精度之间的闭环优化机制，为解决S2R反演的病态问题提供了新范式。

Abstract: Satellite-to-radar (S2R) retrieval estimates ground radar precipitation from geostationary satellite observations, providing a critical solution for precipitation monitoring in radar-sparse regions. However, S2R retrieval is intrinsically ill-posed: similar cloud-top radiances can correspond to distinct precipitation regimes, storm organizations, and surface intensities, which are difficult to uniquely determine the underlying meteorological state from local spectral cues alone. Meteorological semantics offer complementary scene-level information that can help resolve this ambiguity. Yet existing static semantic conditioning is often insufficient, as externally predefined semantics cannot adapt to dynamic convective scenes or align with retrieval objectives. To this end, we propose LangRetrieval, a language-guided conditional flow matching (CFM) framework that establishes a closed-loop optimization mechanism between meteorological semantics and retrieval accuracy. Specifically, LangRetrieval consists of two core components: (i) Semantic Warm-up: structured meteorological attributes are injected into the CFM backbone through cross-attention conditioning, enabling continuous semantic guidance throughout the generation trajectory; and (ii) Self-Evolving Semantic Optimization: a lightweight attribute policy is first initialized from vision-language model annotations and subsequently refined via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) using multi-threshold Critical Success Index (CSI) rewards, enabling semantic generation to evolve directly toward improved retrieval accuracy.

</details>
